Ventures
On 22 August 2024, Shanghai One Leaf Cosmetic Co., Ltd. (上海               於2024年8月22日，本公司全資附屬公司上海一
一葉子化妝品有限公司) (“Shanghai One Leaf”), a wholly-owned                         葉子化妝品有限公司（「上海一葉子」）與杭州芒墾

agreement with Hangzhou Mangken Brand Management Co., Ltd.                葉子及杭州芒墾同意成立上海一葉子護膚品有限
(杭州芒墾品牌管理有限公司) (“Hangzhou Mangken”), pursuant to                          公司（「一葉子護膚品」），上海一葉子以現金向一
which Shanghai One Leaf and Hangzhou Mangken agreed to establish          葉子護膚品出資人民幣10.2百萬元，佔一葉子護
Shanghai One Leaf Skincare Products Co., Ltd.* (上海一葉子護膚品有                 膚品初始註冊資本總額的51%。杭州芒墾係一家
限公司) (the “One Leaf Skincare Products”), and Shanghai One Leaf            在中國成立的有限責任公司，於本年報日期分別

accounting for 51% of the total initial registered capital of One Leaf    權（彼等均為執行董事、董事長、首席執行官及
Skincare Products. Hangzhou Mangken is a limited liability company        本公司控股股東呂義雄先生的表兄弟）。根據上
established in the PRC and is owned as to 99.5% by Mr. Lyu Zeqi           市規則，成立一葉子護膚品構成上市規則項下本
(呂澤奇) and 0.5% by Mr. Lyu Zecai (呂澤財), both being cousin of               公司的關連交易。
Mr. Lyu Yixiong (呂義雄), an executive Director, the chairman of the

into a brand with annual gross merchandise volume (GMV) exceeding         億元的品牌。成立一葉子護膚品旨在利用本集團

standards.

essential in providing a framework for the Company to safeguard the         框架以保障本公司股東（「股東」）權益、提升企業
interests of the shareholders of the Company (the “Shareholder(s)”),        價值、制定其業務策略及政策以及提高其透明度
enhance corporate value, formulate its business strategies and              及問責性所必需。
policies, and enhance its transparency and accountability.

of the Company’s corporate governance practices.

The Company is headed by an effective Board which assumes                 本公司由有效的董事會領導，董事會履行領導及

responsible for promoting the Company’s success by directing and          責促進本公司的成功。董事應客觀地作出符合本
supervising the Company’s affairs. Directors take decisions objectively   公司最佳利益的決策。
in the best interests of the Company.


The Board has a balance of skills, experience and diversity of            本公司董事會成員於切合本公司業務所需之技
perspectives appropriate to the requirements of the Company’s             能、經驗及多元觀點中取得平衡，並定期檢討董
business and regularly reviews the contribution required from a           事就履行其對本公司的職責而須作出的貢獻以及
Director to perform his responsibilities to the Company and whether       董事是否付出足夠時間履行與其角色及董事會職
the Director is spending sufficient time performing them that are         責相稱的職責。董事會的執行董事及非執行董事
commensurate with their role and the Board responsibilities. The          （包括獨立非執行董事）組成均衡，使董事會可具
Board includes a balanced composition of executive directors              備有力的獨立元素，有效作出獨立判斷。
and non-executive directors (including independent non-executive
directors) so that there is a strong independent element on the Board,
which can effectively exercise independent judgement.

Chairman and Chief Executive Officer                                      董事長及首席執行官
Code Provision C.2.1 of the CG Code states that the roles of              企業管治守則的守則條文 C.2.1 列明董事長與首
chairman and chief executive officer should be separate and should        席執行官的角色應有區分，不應由一人同時兼
not be performed by the same individual. Mr. Lyu Yixiong is both the      任。呂義雄先生兼任本公司董事長及首席執行
chairman of the Board and the chief executive officer of the Company.     官。儘管與企業管治守則的守則條文 C.2.1 有所
Notwithstanding the deviation from Code Provision C.2.1 of the CG         偏離，惟鑒於呂義雄先生對本集團業務擁有豐富
Code, given Mr. Lyu Yixiong’s extensive knowledge and experience          知識及經驗，董事會認為，本公司董事長及首席
of the Group’s business, the Board considers that vesting the roles       執行官的角色由一人同時兼任有利於確保本集團
of both chairman of the Board and chief executive officer of the          內部的領導一致，並為本集團實現更有效及更有
Company in the same person brings the benefit of ensuring consistent      效率的整體策略規劃。董事會相信，現時安排下
leadership within the Group and enabling more effective and efficient     的權力與權限平衡不會受到損害，而此架構將使
overall strategic planning for the Group. The Board believes that the     本公司能夠迅速有效地作出決策並予以執行。
balance of power and authority for the present arrangement will not
be impaired and this structure will enable the Company to make and
implement decisions promptly and efficiently.


The Board will nevertheless continue to review the structure from time    儘管如此，董事會將繼續不時檢討有關架構，並
to time and consider the appropriate move to take when appropriate.       考慮在適當時採取適當舉措。

SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS                                                         股東權利

Convening an Extraordinary General Meeting                                   召集臨時股東會
Pursuant to Article 47 of the Articles, Shareholders severally or jointly    根據公司章程第47條，單獨或合計持有本公司股
holding ten percent (10%) or more of shares of the Company have the          份百分之十(10%)或以上的股東有權向董事會請
right to request the Board of Directors to convene an extraordinary          求召開臨時股東會，並應當以書面形式向董事會
general meeting and such request shall be made in writing to the             提出。董事會應當根據法律、行政法規及公司章
Board of Directors. The Board of Directors shall, in accordance with         程的規定，在收到請求後十(10)日內提出同意或
laws, administrative regulations and the Articles of Association, reply      不同意召開臨時股東會的書面反饋意見。
in writing as to whether the Board of Directors agrees or disagrees
on the convening of the extraordinary general meeting within ten (10)
days upon the receipt of the requisition.


If the Board of Directors agrees to convene an extraordinary general         董事會同意召開臨時股東會的，應當在董事會決
meeting, a convening notice shall be issued within five (5) days after       議案後的五(5)日內發出召開大會的通知，通知中
the resolution of the Board of Directors. Any modification to the            對原請求的變更，應當徵得相關股東的同意。
original requisition(s) shall be approved by the relevant shareholders.


If the Board of Directors disagrees to convene the extraordinary             董事會不同意召開臨時股東會，或者在收到請求
general meeting, or fails to issue a reply within ten (10) days upon         後十(10)日內未作出反饋的，單獨或合計持有本
receipt of the requisition(s), shareholders severally or jointly holding     公司股份百分之十(10%)或以上的股東有權向監
ten percent (10%) or more of the shares of the Company are entitled          事會提議召開臨時股東會，並應當以書面形式向
to propose to the Supervisory Committee to convene an extraordinary          監事會提出請求。
general meeting, and shall make such requests in writing to the
Supervisory Committee.


If the Supervisory Committee agrees to convene the extraordinary             監事會同意召開臨時股東會的，應在收到請求後
general meeting, a convening notice will be issued within five (5) days      五(5)日內發出召開大會的通知，通知中對原請求
upon the receipt of the request, and any modification to the original        的變更，應當徵得相關股東的同意。
requisition(s) shall be approved by relevant shareholders.


In the event that the Supervisory Committee fails to issue the               監事會未在規定期限內發出召開通知的，視為監
convening notice within the prescribed period, the Supervisory               事會不召集或主持股東會，連續九十(90)日以上
Committee will be deemed as not to convene or preside the general            單獨或合計持有本公司股份百分之十(10%)或以
meeting. Shareholders severally or jointly holding ten percent (10%) or      上的股東可以自行召集及主持股東會。
more of the shares of the Company for consecutive ninety (90) days
may convene the general meeting and preside over on their own.

Putting Forward Proposals at General Meetings                               於股東會上提呈提議
Pursuant to the Article 52 of the Articles, Shareholders individually or    根據公司章程第 52 條，單獨或者合計持有公司
collectively holding one percent (1%) or more of the total voting shares    有表決權的股份總數百分之一(1%)以上股份的股
of the Company shall be entitled to propose new resolutions to the          東，有權在股東會召開十(10)日前以書面形式向
Company in writing which should be submitted to the convener ten            公司提出新的提案並提交召集人，股東會召集人
(10) days prior to the convening of the general meeting. The convener       應在收到提案後兩(2)日內發出股東會補充通知，
of the general meeting shall issue a supplemental notice of general         公告臨時提案的內容，通知其他股東，並將提案
meeting to other shareholders within two (2) days of the receipt of         中屬於股東會職責範圍內的事項，列入該次會議
such proposal to announce the contents of the provisional proposal          的議程提交股東會審議。
and incorporate such newly proposed matters falling within the scope
of duties of the general meeting into the agenda of such meeting for
consideration at the general meeting.

     終止：                         合資合作協議的任何一方於下列情況發生時均有權終止合作：


                                 (1) the accumulative losses of the Joint Venture exceeding RMB50 million;
                                 (1) 合資公司的累計虧損超過人民幣50百萬元；


                                 (2) the Joint Venture recording losses for three consecutive years; and
                                 (2) 合資公司連續三年錄得虧損；及


                                 (3) the Joint Venture failing to reach an annual sales revenue of RMB1 billion (tax
                                     inclusive) within the third year after the establishment of the Joint Venture.
                                 (3) 合資公司於其成立後第三年內的年度銷售收入未能達致人民幣10億元（含稅）。

Non-competition:               Hangzhou Mangken and its actual controller, Mr. Lyu Zeqi, undertake that, during
                               the term of operation of the Joint Venture and before the annual sales revenue (tax
                               inclusive) of the Joint Venture exceeds RMB2 billion for two consecutive years, other
                               than the business conducted by them prior to the execution of the Joint Venture
                               and Cooperation Agreement, Hangzhou Mangken and its actual controller shall
                               not, directly or indirectly, engage in any business that competes with One Leaf (the
                               “Competing Business”), hold any position or equity interest in the entities that
                               conduct the Competing Business, or, directly or indirectly, invest in, participate
                               in, assist, provide consulting or advisory services to or otherwise engage in the
                               Competing Business in any manner.
不競爭：                           杭州芒墾及其實際控制人呂澤奇先生承諾，於合資公司的經營期限內及在合資公司的
                               年度銷售收入（含稅）未連續兩年超過人民幣20億元前，除彼等於簽立合資合作協議前
                               經營的業務外，杭州芒墾及其實際控制人不得直接或間接從事與一葉子構成競爭的任
                               何業務（「競爭業務」）、於進行競爭業務的實體中擔任任何職位或持有任何股權，或以
                               任何方式直接或間接投資、參與、協助、提供諮詢或顧問服務或以其他方式從事競爭
                               業務。

Special arrangement:           During the term of operation of the Joint Venture, if the sales revenue (tax inclusive)
                               of facial skincare products under One Leaf in the channels that the Joint Venture
                               operates exceeds RMB2 billion and the net profit margin after tax is no less than
                               10%, and Hangzhou Mangken intends to dispose part of its equity interest in the
                               Joint Venture, Shanghai One Leaf and Hangzhou Mangken shall negotiate on the
                               reasonable price, transaction structure, transaction method, etc. of such acquisition.
                               Shanghai One Leaf and the Company may acquire no more than 10% of the equity
                               interest in the Joint Venture held by Hangzhou Mangken at a reasonable price after
                               completion of necessary internal review and decision-making procedures, subject to
                               the requirements under the then applicable laws and regulations, Listing Rules and
                               relevant provisions of securities regulatory authorities.
特別安排：                          於合資公司的經營期限內，倘一葉子旗下的面部護膚產品在合資公司運營渠道的銷售
                               收入（含稅）超過人民幣20億元及除稅後淨利率不低於10%，且杭州芒墾擬出售其於合
                               資公司的部分股權，則上海一葉子及杭州芒墾須就有關收購的合理價格、交易架構及
                               交易方法等進行磋商。上海一葉子及本公司可於完成必要的內部審閱及決策程序後以
                               合理價格收購杭州芒墾於合資公司所持有不超過10%的股權，惟須遵守當時適用法律
                               法規、上市規則及證券監管機構相關條文項下的規定。

                                     生的銷售收入（含稅）除以一葉子旗下所產生的總銷售收入（含稅）的百分比）計算。

      “Logistics and Warehousing Service Agreement”), pursuant                   合資公司提供物流及倉儲服務。物流及倉

      and warehousing services to the Joint Venture. The principal

      訂約方：                          (1) 杭州融恒（為其本身及代表其附屬公司）；及

      Pricing principles:           The pricing for providing logistics and warehousing services to the Joint Venture will
                                    be calculated by the following formula, which was determined on normal commercial
                                    terms at arm’s length negotiation, taking into account the estimated costs and a
                                    proper level of premium for providing the logistics and warehousing services:
      定價原則：                         向合資公司提供物流及倉儲服務的定價將按以下公式計算，該定價乃經計及提供物流

                                    (Actual costs incurred by Hangzhou Rongheng + Allocated costs incurred by
                                    Hangzhou Rongheng) × (1+10%)
                                   （杭州融恒產生的實際成本+杭州融恒產生的已分攤成本）×(1+10%)

Basis for opinion                                                         意見基準

We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards             我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審計準則
on Auditing (“HKSAs”) as issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities       （「香港審計準則」）進行審計。我們在該等準則
under those standards are further described in the Auditor’s              下承擔的責任已在我們的報告「核數師就審計綜

of the consolidated financial statements                                    擔的責任

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the         我們的目標是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由
consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material         於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理保
misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s      證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們按
report that includes our opinion. Our report is made solely to you, as      香港公司條例第405 條之規定僅對全體成員作出
a body, in accordance with section 405 of the Hong Kong Companies           報告，除此以外，本報告並無其他用途。我們不
Ordinance, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility        會就本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔任
towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this    何責任。
report.


Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance but is not a              合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照香港
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will             審計準則進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在
always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements         時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，
can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,               如果合理預期錯誤陳述單獨或匯總起來可能影響
individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to      綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的
influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these       經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
consolidated financial statements.


As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise                   在根據香港審計準則進行審計的過程中，我們運
professional judgement and maintain professional scepticism                 用專業判斷，保持專業懷疑態度。我們亦：
throughout the audit. We also:


•     Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the         •   識別及評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜合財
      consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or error,         務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設計及
      design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks,            進行審計程序以應對這些風險，以及獲取
      and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and appropriate              充足及適當的審計憑證，作為我們意見的
      to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a           基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄
      material misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for             意遺漏、虛假陳述，或淩駕於內部控制之
      one resulting from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery,        上，因此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯
      intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of             誤陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導致
      internal control.                                                         的重大錯誤陳述的風險。


•     Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the audit     •   了解與審計相關的內部控制，以設計適當
      in order to design audit procedures that are appropriate in the           的審計程序，但目的並非對貴集團內部控
      circumstances, but not for the purpose of expressing an opinion           制的有效性發表意見。
      on the effectiveness of the Group’s internal control.

2. Accounting Policies                                                       2. 會計政策

      2.1 Basis of Preparation                                                2.1 編製基準
            These financial statements have been prepared in                    該等財務報表已按照國際會計準則理
            accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards (which                    事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒佈
            include all International Financial Reporting Standards,            之國際財務報告準則會計準則（包括
            International Accounting Standards (“IASs”) and                     所有國際財務報告準則、國際會計準
            Interpretations) issued by International Accounting                 則（「國際會計準則」）及詮釋）及香港
            Standards Board (the “IASB”) and the disclosure                     公司條例的披露規定編製。該等財務
            requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                  報表採用歷史成本慣例編製，惟已按
            They have been prepared under the historical cost                   公平值計量的按公平值計入損益的金
            convention, except for financial assets at fair value through       融資產除外。該等財務報表乃以人
            profit or loss which have been measured at fair value.              民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，除非另有說
            These financial statements are presented in Renminbi                明，否則所有金額均四捨五入至最接
            (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to the nearest                   近的千位整數。
            thousand except when otherwise indicated.


            Basis of consolidation                                              綜合基準
            The consolidated financial statements include the                   綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公
            financial statements of the Company and its subsidiaries            司（統稱「本集團」）截至2024年12月
            (collectively referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended        31 日止年度的財務報表。附屬公司
            31 December 2024. A subsidiary is an entity (including a            為本公司直接或間接控制的實體（包
            structured entity), directly or indirectly, controlled by the       括結構性實體）。當本集團對參與投
            Company. Control is achieved when the Group is exposed,             資對象業務的浮動回報承擔風險或享
            or has rights, to variable returns from its involvement with        有權利以及能透過對投資對象的權力
            the investee and has the ability to affect those returns            （即本集團獲賦予現有能力以主導投
            through its power over the investee (i.e., existing rights          資對象相關活動的既存權利）影響該
            that give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant      等回報時，即取得控制權。
            activities of the investee).


            Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting         一般情況下，有一個推定，即多數投
            rights results in control. When the Company has, less than          票權形成控制權。倘本公司直接或間
            a majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee,          接擁有投資對象少於過半數的投票權
            the Group considers all relevant facts and circumstances            或類似權利，則本集團於評估其是否
            in assessing whether it has power over an investee,                 擁有對投資對象的權力時會考慮一切
            including:                                                          相關事實及情況，包括：


            (a)    the contractual arrangement with the other vote              (a)   與投資對象其他投票持有人的
                   holders of the investee;                                           合約安排；


            (b)    rights arising from other contractual arrangements;          (b)   其他合約安排所產生的權利；
                   and                                                                及


            (c)    the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.       (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

   2.1 Basis of Preparation (Continued)                                  2.1 編製基準（續）
       Basis of consolidation (Continued)                                   綜合基準（續）
       The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared            附屬公司的財務報表乃採用貫徹一致
       for the same reporting period as the Company, using                  的會計政策就與本公司相同的報告期
       consistent accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries          間而編製。附屬公司的業績由本集團
       are consolidated from the date on which the Group                    取得控制權當日起綜合入賬，並繼續
       obtains control, and continue to be consolidated until the           綜合入賬至該控制權終止當日。
       date that such control ceases.


       Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive             損益及其他全面收入的各組成部分歸
       income are attributed to the owners of the parent of the             屬於本集團母公司擁有人及非控股權
       Group and to the non-controlling interests, even if this             益，即使此舉引致非控股權益出現虧
       results in the non-controlling interests having a deficit            絀結餘。所有集團內公司間資產及負
       balance. All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity,             債、權益、收入、開支以及與本集團
       income, expenses and cash flows relating to transactions             成員公司之間交易有關的現金流量均
       between members of the Group are eliminated in full on               於綜合入賬時悉數抵銷。
       consolidation.


       The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an                   倘事實及情況反映上述三項控制權因
       investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there are          素中一項或多項有變，則本集團會重
       changes to one or more of the three elements of control              估是否仍然控制投資對象。附屬公司
       described above. A change in the ownership interest of a             擁有權權益的變動（並無失去控制權）
       subsidiary, without a loss of control, is accounted for as           入賬為權益交易。
       an equity transaction.


       If the Group loses control over a subsidiary, it                     倘本集團失去對一間附屬公司的控制
       derecognises the related assets (including goodwill),                權，則其終止確認相關資產（包括商
       liabilities, any non-controlling interest and the exchange           譽）、負債、任何非控股權益及累計
       fluctuation reserve cumulative; and recognises the                   匯兌變動儲備；及於損益中確認任何
       fair value of any investment retained and any resulting              保留投資的公平值及任何因而產生的
       surplus or deficit in profit or loss. The Group’s share of           盈餘或虧絀。先前於其他全面收入內
       components previously recognised in other comprehensive              確認的本集團應佔部分按假設本集團
       income is reclassified to profit or loss or retained profits,        直接出售相關資產或負債所須採用的
       as appropriate, on the same basis as would be required if            相同基準，視乎情況重新分類至損益
       the Group had directly disposed of the related assets or             或保留盈利。
       liabilities.

2. Accounting Policies (Continued)                                         2. 會計政策（續）
      2.4 Material Accounting Policies                                      2.4 主要會計政策
            Investments in associates and joint ventures                      於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
            An associate is an entity in which the Group has a                聯營公司指本集團長期持有一般不少
            long-term interest of generally not less than 20% of the          於20%股本投票權並對其有重大影響
            equity voting rights and over which it has significant            力的實體。重大影響力指參與投資對
            influence. Significant influence is the power to participate      象的財務及經營政策的權力，而並非
            in the financial and operating policy decisions of the            控制或共同控制該等政策。
            investee, but is not control or joint control over those
            policies.


            A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby            合營企業為一項合營安排，據此，擁
            the parties that have joint control of the arrangement            有安排共同控制權的人士均有權享有
            have rights to the net assets of the joint venture. Joint         合營企業的資產淨值。共同控制為合
            control is the contractually agreed sharing of control of an      約協定應佔安排控制權，其僅在相關
            arrangement, which exists only when decisions about the           活動決策必須獲應佔控制權的人士一
            relevant activities require the unanimous consent of the          致同意方存在。
            parties sharing control.


            The Group’s investments in associates and joint ventures          本集團於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
            are stated in the consolidated statement of financial             以本集團應佔資產淨值減任何減值虧
            position at the Group’s share of net assets under the             損，按權益會計法於綜合財務狀況表
            equity method of accounting, less any impairment losses.          列賬。


            The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and             本集團分佔聯營公司及合營企業收購
            other comprehensive income of associates and joint                後業績及其他全面收入分別計入綜合
            ventures is included in the consolidated statement of             損益表及綜合其他全面收入表。此
            profit or loss and consolidated other comprehensive               外，倘已於聯營公司或合營企業權益
            income, respectively. In addition, when there has been a          中直接確認變動，則本集團將於綜合
            change recognised directly in the equity of the associate         權益變動表中確認其應佔的任何變動
            or joint venture, the Group recognises its share of any           （倘適用）。本集團與其聯營公司或合
            changes, when applicable, in the consolidated statement           營企業之間的交易產生的未變現收益
            of changes in equity. Unrealised gains and losses resulting       及虧損將予對銷，惟以本集團於聯營
            from transactions between the Group and its associates            公司或合營企業的投資為限，除非未
            or joint ventures are eliminated to the extent of the             變現虧損提供證據顯示已轉讓資產出
            Group’s investment in the associates or joint ventures,           現減值。收購聯營公司或合營企業所
            except where unrealised losses provide evidence of an             產生的商譽計入本集團於聯營公司或
            impairment of the assets transferred. Goodwill arising from       合營企業投資的部分。
            the acquisition of associates or joint ventures is included
            as part of the Group’s investments in associates or joint
            ventures.

       not remeasured. Instead, the investment continues to be               計量保留權益。相反，投資將繼續根
       accounted for under the equity method. In all other cases,            據權益法入賬。在所有其他情況下，
       upon loss of significant influence over the associate or joint        一旦本集團喪失對聯營公司的重大影

       Business combinations and goodwill                                    業務合併及商譽


       Business combinations are accounted for using the                     業務合併乃以購買法入賬。轉讓之代
       acquisition method. The consideration transferred is                  價乃以收購日期的公平值計量，該公
       measured at the acquisition date fair value which is                  平值為本集團轉讓的資產於收購日期
       the sum of the acquisition date fair values of assets                 的公平值、本集團自被收購方的前度
       transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed by the Group            擁有人承擔的負債，及本集團發行以
       to the former owners of the acquiree and the equity                   換取被收購方控制權的股本權益的總
       interests issued by the Group in exchange for control of              和。於各業務合併中，本集團選擇是
       the acquiree. For each business combination, the Group                否以公平值或被收購方可識別資產淨
       elects whether to measure the non-controlling interests in            值的應佔比例，計量於被收購方的非
       the acquiree at fair value or at the proportionate share of           控股權益。非控股權益的一切其他成
       the acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All other components          份乃按公平值計量。收購相關成本於
       of non-controlling interests are measured at fair value.              產生時列為開支。
       Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.


       The Group determines that it has acquired a business                  倘所獲得的一組活動及資產包括一項
       when the acquired set of activities and assets include an             投入及一個實質性過程，兩者共同顯
       input and a substantive process that together significantly           著促進創造產出的能力，則本集團釐
       contribute to the ability to create outputs.                          定其已收購一項業務。


       When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the                   當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合
       financial assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate              約條款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相
       classification and designation in accordance with the                 關條件，評估將承接的金融資產及負
       contractual terms, economic circumstances and pertinent               債，以作出適合的分類及標示，其中
       conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the              包括將被收購方主合約中的嵌入式衍
       separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of               生工具進行分離。
       the acquiree.

            classified as equity is not remeasured and its subsequent           其後結算於權益內入賬。
            settlement is accounted for within equity.


            Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the excess            商譽初步按成本計量，即已轉讓代

            the acquiree over the identifiable net assets acquired and          淨值及所承擔負債的差額。如該代價
            liabilities assumed. If the sum of this consideration and           及其他項目總額低於所收購資產淨值
            other items is lower than the fair value of the net assets          的公平值，於再評估後其差額將於損
            acquired, the difference is, after reassessment, recognised         益內確認為議價收購收益。
            in profit or loss as a gain on bargain purchase.


            After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost less        於初步確認後，商譽按成本減任何累
            any accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested               計減值虧損計量。商譽須每年作減值

            changes in circumstances indicate that the carrying                 賬面值有可能減值，則會更頻密地進

       of cash-generating units) is less than the carrying amount,          面值，則減值虧損予以確認。已就商
       an impairment loss is recognised. An impairment loss                 譽確認的減值虧損不會於其後期間撥
       recognised for goodwill is not reversed in a subsequent              回。
       period.

       Fair value measurement                                               公平值計量
       The Group measures its debt investments at fair value                本集團於各報告期間末透過其他全面
       through other comprehensive income at the end of each                收入按公平值計量其債務投資。公平
       reporting period. Fair value is the price that would be              值乃市場參與者之間於計量日進行的
       received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in         有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓負
       an orderly transaction between market participants at the            債所支付的價格。公平值乃假設出售
       measurement date. The fair value measurement is based                資產或轉讓負債的交易於該資產或負
       on the presumption that the transaction to sell the asset            債的主要市場（倘無主要市場，則為
       or transfer the liability takes place either in the principal        對該資產或負債最有利的市場）進行
       market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a            而計量。本集團必須可於該主要市場
       principal market, in the most advantageous market for the            或最有利市場進行交易。資產或負債
       asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous           的公平值乃採用市場參與者為資產或
       market must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of            負債定價時所用的假設計量，並假設
       an asset or a liability is measured using the assumptions            市場參與者按本身最佳經濟利益行
       that market participants would use when pricing the asset            事。
       or liability, assuming that market participants act in their
       economic best interest.

            Fair value measurement (Continued)                                   公平值計量（續）
            A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes              非金融資產的公平值計量，須衡量市
            into account a market participant’s ability to generate              場參與者最大程度及最佳使用該資產
            economic benefits by using the asset in its highest and              得到經濟利益的能力，或將該資產售
            best use or by selling it to another market participant that         予另一可最大程度及最佳使用該資產
            would use the asset in its highest and best use.                     的市場參與者而獲得經濟利益的能
                                                                                 力。


            The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate             本集團採用在有關情況下屬適當的估
            in the circumstances and for which sufficient data are               值技術，且有充足數據可計量公平
            available to measure fair value, maximising the use of               值、盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據同
            relevant observable inputs and minimising the use of                 時盡量避免使用不可觀察輸入數據。
            unobservable inputs.


            All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured          所有於財務資料中計量或披露公平值
            or disclosed in the financial information are categorised            的資產及負債，按對公平值計量整體
            within the fair value hierarchy, described as follows, based         重要的最低水平參數分類為下述的公
            on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value      平值層級：
            measurement as a whole:


            Level 1    –    based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in               第一級 － 基 於 相 同 資 產 或 負 債 於
                            active markets for identical assets or                       活躍市場的報價（未經調
                            liabilities                                                  整）


            Level 2    –    based on valuation techniques for which              第二級 － 基 於 其 最 低 水 平 參 數 對
                            the lowest level input that is significant to                公平值計量有重要性且
                            the fair value measurement is observable,                    可以直接或間接觀察的
                            either directly or indirectly                                估值技術


            Level 3    –    based on valuation techniques for which              第三級 － 基 於 其 最 低 水 平 參 數 對
                            the lowest level input that is significant to                公平值計量有重要性且
                            the fair value measurement is unobservable                   不可以觀察的估值技術


            For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the financial      對於財務資料內按經常性基準確認的
            information on a recurring basis, the Group determines               資產及負債，本集團於報告期間末重

            hierarchy by reassessing categorisation (based on the                要的最低水平參數作準），以釐定公

            period.

            Other intangible assets with finite lives are subsequently           無形資產隨後在可使用的經濟年期內

            intangible asset may be impaired. The amortisation period            使用年期的無形資產的攤銷期和攤銷

            finite useful life are reviewed at least at each financial year

       Derecognition of financial assets                                     終止確認金融資產
       A financial asset (or, where applicable, a part of a financial        金融資產（或如適用，一項金融資產
       asset or part of a group of similar financial assets) is              的一部分或一組同類金融資產的一
       primarily derecognised (i.e., removed from the Group’s                部分）主要在下列情況下將被終止確
       consolidated statement of financial position) when:                   認（即從本集團的綜合財務狀況表移
                                                                             除）：


       •     the rights to receive cash flows from the asset have            •     收取該項資產所得現金流量的
             expired; or                                                           權利經已屆滿；或


       •     the Group has transferred its rights to receive cash            •     本集團已轉讓其收取該項資產
             flows from the asset or has assumed an obligation                     所得現金流量的權利，或已根
             to pay the received cash flows in full without material               據一項「轉付」安排承擔責任，
             delay to a third party under a “pass-through”                         在無重大延誤之情況下，將所
             arrangement; and either (a) the Group has                             收到的現金流量全數付予第三
             transferred substantially all the risks and rewards of                方；及(a)本集團已轉讓該項資
             the asset, or (b) the Group has neither transferred                   產的絕大部分風險及回報；或
             nor retained substantially all the risks and rewards of               (b)本集團並無轉讓或保留該項
             the asset, but has transferred control of the asset.                  資產絕大部分風險及回報，但
                                                                                   已轉讓該項資產的控制權。


       When the Group has transferred its rights to receive cash             本集團凡轉讓其收取該項資產所得現
       flows from an asset or has entered into a pass-through                金流量的權利或已訂立轉付安排時，
       arrangement, it evaluates if and to what extent it has                其評估是否已保留該資產擁有權的風
       retained the risk and rewards of ownership of the asset.              險及回報以及有關程度。倘並無轉讓
       When it has neither transferred nor retained substantially            亦無保留該項資產的絕大部分風險
       all the risks and rewards of the asset nor transferred                及回報，及並無轉讓該項資產的控制
       control of the asset, the Group continues to recognise the            權，則本集團將按其持續涉及該項資
       transferred asset to the extent of the Group’s continuing             產的程度持續確認已轉讓資產。在此
       involvement. In that case, the Group also recognises                  情況下，本集團亦確認相關負債。已
       an associated liability. The transferred asset and the                轉讓資產及相關負債乃按可反映本集
       associated liability are measured on a basis that reflects            團已保留的權利及義務作計量。
       the rights and obligations that the Group has retained.

            Impairment of financial assets                                     金融資產減值
            The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit              本集團就並非按公平值計入損益持有
            losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair          的所有債務工具確認預期信貸虧損撥
            value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the                備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根據合約應
            difference between the contractual cash flows due in               付的合約現金流量與本集團預期收取
            accordance with the contract and all the cash flows                的所有現金流量之間的差額，按原有
            that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an                實際利率相近的差額貼現。預期現金
            approximation of the original effective interest rate. The         流量將包括同為合約條款組成部分的
            expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale          自銷售所持有抵押品所得的現金流量
            of collateral held or other credit enhancements that are           或其他提升信貸物品。
            integral to the contractual terms.


            General approach                                                   一般方式
            ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures            預期信貸虧損於兩個階段獲確認。就
            for which there has not been a significant increase in credit      自初步確認起信貸風險並無顯著增加
            risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit       的信貸風險而言，預期信貸虧損提供
            losses that result from default events that are possible           予因未來十二個月內可能發生的違約
            within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those              事件而導致的信貸虧損（十二個月預
            credit exposures for which there has been a significant            期信貸虧損）。就自初步確認起經已
            increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss          顯著增加的信貸風險而言，不論何時
            allowance is required for credit losses expected over the          發生違約事件，於餘下風險年期內的
            remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of      信貸虧損均須計提虧損撥備（全期預
            the default (a lifetime ECL).                                      期信貸虧損）。


            At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                 於各報告日期，本集團評估自初步確
            the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased            認後金融工具的信貸風險是否有大幅
            significantly since initial recognition. When making the           增加。當作出評估時，本集團比較於
            assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default               報告日期金融工具發生違約風險與於
            occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting          初步確認日期金融工具發生違約風
            date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial         險，並考慮於沒有過度成本或努力的
            instrument as at the date of initial recognition and               情況下可得的合理及可支持數據，包
            considers reasonable and supportable information that is           括過往及前瞻性資料。倘合約付款逾
            available without undue cost or effort, including historical       期超過30日以上，則本集團認為信貸
            and forward-looking information. The Group considers that          風險大幅增加。
            there has been a significant increase in credit risk when
            contractual payments are more than 30 days past due.

            simplified approach, the Group does not track changes in              集團不會追蹤信貸風險的變動，而是

            credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors          據債權人及經濟環境的前瞻性因素作

       Derecognition of financial liabilities                                 終止確認金融負債
       A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation              當金融負債的責任已履行、取消或屆
       under the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.            滿時，金融負債會被終止確認。

       from the same lender on substantially different terms,                 人借出，而條款有重大不同的金融負
       or the terms of an existing liability are substantially                債所取代，或當現時負債的條款被重
       modified, such an exchange or modification is treated as               大修訂，該取代或修訂被視為對原有
       a derecognition of the original liability and a recognition of         負債的終止確認及對新負債的確認，
       a new liability, and the difference between the respective             而各自賬面值的差額於損益確認。
       carrying amounts is recognised in profit or loss.

            Inventories                                                       存貨
            Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net               存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較
            realisable value. Cost is determined on a weighted                低者列賬。成本按加權平均成本基準

            and finished goods, comprises direct materials, direct            本包括直接材料、直接勞工及適當比
            labour and an appropriate proportion of overheads. Net            例的間接成本。可變現淨值按估計售
            realisable value is based on estimated selling prices less        價減完成及出售將產生的任何估計成
            any estimated costs to be incurred to completion and              本計算。
            disposal.

            position comprise cash on hand and at banks, and                  括手頭及銀行現金以及到期日一般在
            short-term highly liquid deposits with a maturity of              三個月內的短期高流動性存款，該等
            generally within three months that are readily convertible        存款可隨時兌換已知款額的現金及承
            into known amounts of cash, subject to an insignificant           受的價值變動風險甚微，且持作滿足
            risk of changes in value and held for the purpose of              短期現金承擔。
            meeting short-term cash commitments.


            For the purpose of the consolidated statement of cash             就綜合現金流量表而言，現金及現金

            and at banks, and short-term deposits as defined above,           界定的短期存款，但不包括須應要求
            less bank overdrafts which are repayable on demand and            償還及構成本集團現金管理的一部分
            form an integral part of the Group’s cash management.             的銀行透支。


            Provisions                                                        撥備
            A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal        撥備乃於本集團因以往的事件導致現
            or constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and       行的責任（不論法定或推定）產生，
            it is probable that a future outflow of resources will be         並將有可能需要於日後作出資源流出
            required to settle the obligation, provided that a reliable       以履行責任時確認，惟須能可靠地估
            estimate can be made of the amount of the obligation.             計有關責任的金額。


            When the effect of discounting is material, the amount            倘折現的影響重大，確認為撥備的金
            recognised for a provision is the present value at the end        額則為預期須用作履行責任的日後開
            of the reporting period of the future expenditures expected       支於報告期末的現值。折現的現值因
            to be required to settle the obligation. The increase in the      時間流逝而產生的升幅，乃於損益中
            discounted present value amount arising from the passage          列作財務成本。
            of time is included in finance costs in profit or loss.

       Income tax                                                          所得稅

      management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions            斷、估計及假設，這會影響收入、開支、
      that affect the reported amounts of revenues, expenses, assets      資產及負債的列報數額及其隨附披露以及
      and liabilities, and their accompanying disclosures, and the        或然負債的披露。該等假設及估計的不確
      disclosure of contingent liabilities. Uncertainty about these       定性或會導致日後須就受影響的資產或負
      assumptions and estimates could result in outcomes that could       債賬面值作出重大調整。
      require a material adjustment to the carrying amounts of the
      assets or liabilities affected in the future.

      The key assumptions concerning the future and other key             對導致下個財政年度內資產及負債賬面值
      sources of estimation uncertainty at the end of the reporting       作出重大調整構成重大風險的有關未來的
      period, that have a significant risk of causing a material          主要假設以及於報告期末時估計不確定因
      adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities        素的其他主要來源載述於下。
      within the next financial year, are described below.


      Variable consideration for returns and rebates                      退貨及回扣的可變代價
      The Group estimates variable consideration to be included in the    本集團就出售附帶退貨權利及回扣的產品
      transaction price for the sale of products with rights of return    計入交易價的可變代價作出估計。
      and rebates.


      The Group has developed a statistical model for forecasting         本集團為預測銷售退貨及回扣已制訂統計
      sales returns and rebates. The model used the historical            模式。該模式利用每項產品過往的退貨數
      return data of each product to estimate with expected return        據估計預期退貨百分比，再應用有關百分
      percentages. These percentages are applied to determine the         比釐定可變代價的預期價值。經驗與過往
      expected value of the variable consideration. Any significant       退貨模式之間如有重大變動，均將對本集
      changes in experience as compared to historical return pattern      團估計的預期退貨百分比構成影響。
      will impact the expected return percentages estimated by the
      Group.


      The Group’s expected rebates are analysed on a per customer         本集團按每位客戶，就存在若干限額的合
      basis for contracts that are subject to some thresholds.            約分析預期回扣。於確定客戶是否可以享
      Determining whether a customer is likely to be entitled to a        有回扣時，會視客戶過往回扣權利及至今
      rebate depends on the customer’s historical rebate entitlement,     累計售出及付款收取情況而定。
      sell out and payment collection to date.

      default rates, forecast economic conditions and ECLs is              預期信貸虧損之間關連性進行的評估屬重

      changes in circumstances and forecast economic conditions.           預測經濟狀況相當敏感。本集團過往信貸
      The Group’s historical credit loss experience and forecast           虧損經驗及預測經濟狀況亦未必能代表客
      of economic conditions may also not be representative of a           戶日後的實際違約情況。有關本集團貿易
      customer’s actual default in the future. The information about       應收款項的預期信貸虧損的資料於財務報

3. Significant Accounting Judgements                                    3. 重大會計判斷及估計（續）
   and Estimates (Continued)

  Estimation uncertainty (Continued)                                      估計不確定性（續）
  Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate                      租賃 － 估算增量借款利率
  The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit           本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利
  in a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing             率 ， 因 此 ， 使 用 增 量 借 款 利 率（「 增 量 借
  rate (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of       款利率」）計量租賃負債。增量借款利率為
  interest that the Group would have to pay to borrow over a              本集團於類似經濟環境中為取得與使用權
  similar term, and with a similar security, the funds necessary          資產價值相近之資產，而以類似抵押品與
  to obtain an asset of a similar value to the right-of-use asset         類似期間借入所需資金應支付之利率。因
  in a similar economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects           此，增量借款利率反映了本集團「應支付」
  what the Group “would have to pay”, which requires estimation           的利率，當無可觀察的利率時（如就並無訂
  when no observable rates are available (such as for subsidiaries        立融資交易之附屬公司而言）或當其需要作
  that do not enter into financing transactions) or when it needs         出調整以反映租賃的條款及條件時（如租賃
  to be adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease         並非以附屬公司功能貨幣計值），則須作出
  (for example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s functional        利率估計。當可觀察輸入數據可用時，本
  currency). The Group estimates the IBR using observable inputs          集團使用可觀察輸入數據（如市場利率）估
  (such as market interest rates) when available and is required to       算增量借款利率並須作出若干實體特定的
  make certain entity-specific estimates (such as the subsidiary’s        估計（如附屬公司的獨立信貸評級）。
  stand-alone credit rating).


  Provision for slow-moving inventories and net realisable                滯銷存貨撥備及存貨的可變現淨值
  value of inventories
  Management reviews the ageing analysis of inventories of                管理層於各報告期末審閱本集團存貨的賬
  the Group at the end of each reporting period, and makes                齡分析，並就滯銷存貨項目計提撥備。管
  a provision for slow-moving inventory items. Management                 理層主要依據最近期的發票價格及現行市
  estimates the net realisable value for such inventories based           況，估計該等存貨的可變現淨值。管理層
  primarily on the latest invoice prices and current market               將存貨撇減至可變現淨值乃按存貨的估計
  conditions. A write-down of inventories to net realisable               可變現淨值作出。對撇減金額的評估需要
  value is made based on the estimated net realisable value of            管理層作出估計及判斷。倘實際結果或未
  inventories. The assessment of the write-down amount requires           來預計結果有別於原先估計，則該等差額
  management’s estimates and judgement. Where the actual                  將影響改變有關估計期間的存貨賬面值及
  outcome or expectation in the future is different from the original     存貨的撇減 ╱ 撥回。
  estimate, such differences will impact the carrying value of
  inventories and the write-down/write-back of inventories in the
  period in which such estimate has been changed.

15. Investment Properties                                                 15. 投資物業

   Industrial properties                                                    工業物業

                                                                                       2024              2023
                                                                                     2024年             2023年

                                                                                   RMB’000           RMB’000
                                                                                   人民幣千元           人民幣千元


    As at 1 January 2024                                 於2024年1月1日                    8,065             9,286
    Depreciation charge                                  折舊費用                         (1,236)           (1,221)


    As at 31 December 2024                               於2024年12月31日                  6,829             8,065


   The Group’s investment properties are measured using a cost              本集團的投資物業採用成本模式計量，並
   model and depreciated to write off their costs net of estimated          按直線法在其估計可使用年期內撇銷其成
   residual values over their estimated useful lives on a straight-line     本（扣除估計殘值）進行折舊。
   basis.

      Industrial properties (Continued)                                     工業物業（續）
      The fair value of the Group’s investment properties as at 31          本集團投資物業於 2024 年及 2023 年 12 月
      December 2024 and 2023 are determined by the valuations               31 日的公平值由獨立估值師艾華迪評估諮
      conducted by Avista Valuation Advisory Ltd, an independent            詢有限公司進行的估值釐定。根據估值模
      valuer. Under the valuation models, an income-based approach          式，工業物業採用收入法進行估值。
      has been adopted for industrial properties.


      The income approach is based on the rental income of the              收入法乃基於現有租賃所得及 ╱ 或於現有
      properties derived from the existing leases and/or achievable         市場上可實現的物業租金收入，並已就該
      in the existing market with due allowance for the reversionary        等租賃的復歸收入潛力作出適當撥備，而
      income potential of the leases, which have been then capitalized      有關租賃其後已按適當的資本化比率撥充
      to determine the market value at an appropriate capitalization        資本以釐定市值。
      rate.


      The fair value estimations for the owner-occupied properties          業主自用物業的公平值估計為公平值層級
      were at Level 3 of the fair value hierarchy.                          第三級。


      The fair value measurement is based on the above properties’          公平值計量乃基於上述物業的最高及最佳
      highest and best use, which does not differ from their actual         用途作出，有關用途與彼等的實際用途並
      use.                                                                  無不同。


      During the year ended 31 December 2024, there were no                 於截至2024年12月31日止年度，概無轉入
      transfers into or out of Level 3 or any other level.                  或轉出第三級或任何其他層級。

      in note 16.

31. Reserves                                                               31. 儲備

   The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements                     本集團於本年度及過往年度的儲備金額及
   therein for the current and prior years are presented in the              其變動於綜合權益變動表中呈列。
   consolidated statement of changes in equity.


   Share-based payment reserve                                               以股份為基礎的付款儲備
   The share-based payment reserve represents reserve arisen                 以股份為基礎的付款儲備指股份激勵計劃
   from share incentive schemes. Details of the movements in the             產生的儲備。以股份為基礎的付款儲備的
   share-based payment reserve are set out in the consolidated               變動詳情載於綜合權益變動表。
   statement of changes in equity.


   Share premium                                                             股份溢價
   The increase in the Group’s share premium for the year was a              於年內，本集團的股份溢價增加乃由於財
   result of listing the Company’s shares on the Hong Kong Stock             務報表附註29 所述本公司股份於香港聯交
   Exchange as described in note 29 to the financial statement.              所上市所致。


   Statutory reserve                                                         法定儲備
   In accordance with the Company Law of the People’s Republic               根據《中華人民共和國公司法》，中國境內
   of China, each company in the PRC is required to allocate 10%             各公司須提取法定稅後利潤的 10% 列入法
   of the statutory after tax profits to the statutory reserve until the     定公積金，直至公積金累計總額達到公司
   cumulative total of the reserve reaches 50% of the company’s              註冊資本的 50%。經中國有關政府部門批
   registered capital. Subject to approval from the relevant PRC             准後，法定儲備可用於抵銷任何累計虧損
   authorities, the statutory reserve may be used to offset any              或增加公司的註冊資本。法定儲備不可用
   accumulated losses or increase the registered capital of the              於向中國附屬公司股東分派股息。
   company. The statutory reserve is not available for dividend
   distribution to shareholders of the PRC subsidiaries.


   Capital reserve                                                           資本儲備
   The capital reserve of the Group represents the contribution              本集團的資本儲備指最終控股公司及股東
   from the ultimate holding company and shareholders, business              的出資、受共同控制的業務合併以及代價
   combinations under the common control and the excess of the               超逾所收購非控股權益賬面值的差額。資
   consideration over the carrying amount of the non-controlling             本儲備變動的詳情載於綜合權益變動表。
   interests acquired. Details of the movements in capital reserve
   are set out in the consolidated statement of changes in equity.


   Exchange fluctuation reserve                                              匯兌波動儲備
   The exchange fluctuation reserve represents exchange                      匯兌波動儲備指換算功能貨幣與本集團呈
   differences arising from the translation of the financial statements      列貨幣不同的海外業務的財務報表所產生
   of foreign operations whose functional currencies are different           的匯兌差額。
   from the Group’s presentation currency.

38. F i n a n c i a l R i s k M a n a g e m e n t                          38. 金融風險管理目標及政策
    Objectives and Policies

    The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise cash and            本集團的主要金融工具包括現金及現金等
    cash equivalents, lease liabilities, and interest-bearing bank           價物、租賃負債及計息銀行及其他借款。
    and other borrowings. The main purpose of these financial                該等金融工具的主要目的是為支持本集團
    instruments is to support the Group’s operations. The Group              的營運。本集團有多項其他金融資產及負
    has various other financial assets and liabilities such as trade         債，如其營運中直接產生的貿易應收款項
    receivables and trade payables which arise directly from its             及貿易應付款項。
    operations.


    The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are        本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為外幣風
    foreign currency risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. The board of      險、信貸風險及流動資金風險。董事會審
    directors reviews and agrees policies for managing these risks           閱及批准管理該等風險的政策，詳情概述
    and they are summarised below.                                           如下。


    Foreign currency risk                                                    外幣風險
    The Group’s major businesses are in Mainland China and the               本集團主要業務位於中國內地及大部分交
    majority of the transactions are conducted in RMB. Most of               易以人民幣進行。本集團大部分資產及負
    the Group’s assets and liabilities are denominated in RMB. The           債以人民幣計值。於年內，本集團並無重
    Group does not have material foreign currency risk during the            大外幣風險。
    year.


    Credit risk                                                              信貸風險
    The Group trades only with recognised and creditworthy                   本集團僅與獲認可且信譽良好的第三方進
    third parties. It is the Group’s policy that all customers who           行交易。本集團秉持的政策是，所有希望
    wish to trade on credit terms are subject to credit verification         以信貸方式進行交易的客戶須接受信貸審
    procedures. In addition, receivable balances are monitored on            核程序。此外，持續對應收款項結餘進行
    an ongoing basis and the Group’s exposure to bad debts is not            監控，而本集團承受的壞賬風險並不重大。
    significant.

38. F i n a n c i a l R i s k M a n a g e m e n t                           38. 金融風險管理目標及政策
    Objectives and Policies (Continued)                                       （續）
      Liquidity risk                                                         流動資金風險
      The Group monitors its risk to a shortage of funds using a             本集團採用經常性流動資金計劃工具，監
      recurring liquidity planning tool. This tool considers the maturity    察其資金短缺的風險。此工具會考慮其金

    Capital management                                                         資金管理
    The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are               本集團資金管理的主要目的乃保障本集團
    to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern            有能力持續經營及維持穩健資本比率以支
    and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its             持其業務及使股東價值最大化。
    business and maximise shareholders’ value.


    The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments              本集團管理其資本結構並根據經濟狀況的
    to it in light of changes in economic conditions and the risk              變化及相關資產的風險特徵對其作出調
    characteristics of the underlying assets. To maintain or adjust            整。為保持或調整資本結構，本集團可調
    the capital structure, the Group may adjust the dividend                   整向股東派付的股息，向股東歸還資本或
    payment to shareholders, return capital to shareholders or issue           發行新股。本集團不受任何外界施加的
    new shares. The Group is not subject to any externally imposed             資金規定所限。截至 2024 年 12 月 31 日及
    capital requirements. No change was made in the objectives,                2023年12月31日止年度，資本管理目標、
    policies or processes for managing capital during the years                政策或流程概無任何變動。
    ended 31 December 2024 and 31 December 2023.


    The Group monitors capital using debt to equity ratio, which               本集團採用負債權益比率（即總債務除以總
    is total debt divided by the total equity. Total debt includes             權益）監察資本。總債務包括流動負債及非
    current liabilities and non-current liabilities. Total equity includes     流動負債。總權益包括母公司擁有人應佔
    equity attributable to owners of the parent and non-controlling            權益及非控股權益。
    interests.
